I giocatori a turno muovono sul tabellone di gioco secondo il risultato del tiro di due dadi.     $("#trending-users").on("click", ".js-track", function(e) {
        var $item = $(this),
            type = $item.attr("data-type");
        debugger;
        if($item.hasClass("js-track-avatar")){
            MP.trackAnalyticsEvent(["_trackEvent", "TrendingUsers", "avatar", type]); // tracking
        }else{
            MP.trackAnalyticsEvent(["_trackEvent", "TrendingUsers", "rankings", type]); // tracking
        }
    });

<script>
    // Does the user likes our page? (only for authenticated users)
    MP.thirdParty.facebook.onReady( function(){
        if (MP.thirdParty.facebook.isConnected()) {
            //console.log("User connected on facebook");
            MP.thirdParty.facebook.isFan(function(isFan) {
                if (isFan) {
                    $("#social-fb-like").hide();
                    $("#social-fb-already-like").show();
                }
            });
        }
    });
    // Load facebook fans asynchronously with jsonp to be as fast as we can, doing it by using the SDK is too slow
    var facebookCallback = function(response) {
        if (response instanceof Object && response.hasOwnProperty("likes") ) {
            $("#social-fb").show().find(".js-amount").html(MP.i18n.writeNumber(response.likes));
        } else if (response instanceof Object && response.hasOwnProperty("fan_count") ) {
            $("#social-fb").show().find(".js-amount").html(MP.i18n.writeNumber(response.fan_count));
        }
    };
    // Harcoding result if no data is loaded
    MP.registerEventListener("ready",function() {
        //if($("#social-fb").find(".js-amount").html() == '&nbsp;') $("#social-fb").find(".js-amount").html('1.638.495');
        //!function(d,s,id){var js,fjs=d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0];if(!d.getElementById(id)){js=d.createElement(s);js.id=id;js.src="//platform.twitter.com/widgets.js";fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js,fjs);}}(document,"script","twitter-wjs");
    });
</script>
<script>
    MP.registerEventListener("ready",function() {
        facebookCallback({
            "likes": "2700000",
            "fan_count": "2700000"
        });
    });
</script>

            disclaimerDeniedMarkup = "<div id='minipush-disclaimer' class='notice denied dropdown-menu header-user-dropdown'><span class='thumbnail'><img src='{DISCLAIMERICON}' /></span><div class='n-header'><div class='title'>{TITLE}</div><div class='subtitle'>{TEXT}</div></div><div class='extended'><a href='https://www.minijuegosgratis.com/v3/cdn/assets/208/1571137274208/unblock_notification_it.png' target='_blank'><img src='https://www.minijuegosgratis.com/v3/cdn/assets/208/1571137274208/unblock_notification_it.png' /></a></div><div class='buttons-case'><button id='doOK' type='button' class='btn btn-highlight btn-full callbackOK'>{OKBUTTONTEXT}</button></div></div>",
            //disclaimerIcon = "https://www.minijuegosgratis.com/v3/cdn/assets/251/1570613177251/minipush_notifications_disclaimer_icon.png",
            disclaimerIcon = "https://www.minijuegosgratis.com/v3/cdn/assets/494/1571220460494/minipush_notifications_disclaimer_medium.png",
            disclaimerDeniedIcon = "https://www.minijuegosgratis.com/v3/cdn/assets/798/1570616967798/minipush_notifications_disclaimer_icon_denied.png",
            disclaimerTitle = "Do you want to receive our last updates?",
            disclaimerText = "Subscribe and win all this for free!",
            disclaimerDeniedTitle = "Don't you want to receive our last updates? :(",
            disclaimerDeniedText = "Please unblock our notifications!",
            disclaimerOKButtonText = "OK",
            disclaimerDeniedOKButtonText = "OK",
            disclaimerKOButtonText = "No, Grazie",

            minipushSettings = {
            "clientId": "d.6abad.301a8df",
            "clientApiKey": "feab8b1b11dbf863bb82275590e16143",
            "debug": true,
            "urlServiceWorker": "https://www.minigiochi.com/sw.js",
            "autoSubscribe": false,
            disclaimerHTMLTemplate: disclaimerMarkup,
            disclaimerDeniedHTMLTemplate: disclaimerMarkup,
            disclaimerIcon : disclaimerIcon,
            disclaimerDeniedIcon : disclaimerIcon,
            disclaimerTitle : disclaimerTitle,
            disclaimerText : disclaimerText,
            disclaimerDeniedTitle : disclaimerTitle,
            disclaimerDeniedText : disclaimerText,
            disclaimerOKButtonText : disclaimerOKButtonText,
            disclaimerDeniedOKButtonText : disclaimerOKButtonText,
            disclaimerKOButtonText : disclaimerKOButtonText,
            destroyDisclaimerOnAck: false,
            destroyDisclaimerOnKO: false,
            disclaimerContainerElement: $('#user-widget, #user-widget-no-logged').get(0)
            // disclaimerOnSubscribeOKCallback : function() { $("#pushn-disclaimer-button").fadeOut(function() { this.remove(); })}
        };


        var MinipushReady = new function() {

            var cfg = {
                headerButtonMarkup : "<li id='user-widget-pushn'><a href='#' class='js-dropdown tooltip' data-target='#minipush-disclaimer'>{ICONS}<div class='tooltip-inner ti-mini bottom'>Notifications</div></a></li>",
                iconsMarkup : "<span class='ic-new ic-o-notification header-user-ic'></span><span class='ic-c ic-c-notification header-user-ic active-state'></span>",
                iconsDeniedMarkup: "<span class='ic-c ic-c-notification-alert header-user-ic'></span><span class='ic-c ic-c-notification header-user-ic active-state'></span>",
                injectionTargetSelector : ".pushn-placeholder",
                cookieName : "mp_pushn",
                visitsForAutoTrigger : 3
            };

            var $pushnButton,
                $disclaimer,
                $jsDropdown;

            var isSubscriptionDisguisedMode = true;

            this.onSubscribe = function() {
                if ($pushnButton.length > 0 ) {
                    $pushnButton.fadeOut(function() { this.remove(); });
                }
                MP.trackAnalyticsEvent(["_trackEvent", "Notifications", "Push", "Subscribe"]);
            };

            this.onOKClick = function() {
                closeDisclaimer();
                MP.trackAnalyticsEvent(["_trackEvent", "Notifications", "Push", "SubscribeOKClick"]);
            };

            this.onDeniedOKClick = function() {

                // Close
                closeDisclaimer();

                if(isSubscriptionDisguisedMode) {
                    // Set AND Load new Denied Version
                    minipushSettings.disclaimerDeniedHTMLTemplate= disclaimerDeniedMarkup;
                    minipushSettings.disclaimerDeniedIcon = disclaimerDeniedIcon;
                    minipushSettings.disclaimerDeniedTitle = disclaimerDeniedTitle;
                    minipushSettings.disclaimerDeniedText = disclaimerDeniedText;
                    minipushSettings.disclaimerDeniedOKButtonText = disclaimerDeniedOKButtonText;
                    minipushSettings.disclaimerKOButtonText = disclaimerKOButtonText;
                    minipushSettings.destroyDisclaimerOnAck = false;
                    Minipush.setSettings(minipushSettings);
                    loadDisclaimer();
                    // Auto Show it
                    if ($jsDropdown.length > 0 && !$jsDropdown.hasClass("active")) {
                        $jsDropdown.click();
                    }
                    isSubscriptionDisguisedMode = false;

                }
                MP.trackAnalyticsEvent(["_trackEvent", "Notifications", "Push", "PermissionsDeniedOKClick"]);
            };

            this.onSubscribeKO = function() {
                closeDisclaimer();
                MP.trackAnalyticsEvent(["_trackEvent", "Notifications", "Push", "SubscribeKOClick"]);
            };

            var closeDisclaimer = function() {
                if ($jsDropdown.length > 0 && $jsDropdown.hasClass("active")) {
                    $jsDropdown.click();
                }
            };

            /**
             *
             */
            var loadDisclaimer = function() {
                Minipush.subscription.displaySubscribeRequest(function() {
                        $jsDropdown = $pushnButton.find(".js-dropdown");
                        $disclaimer = $("#minipush-disclaimer");
                        if ($jsDropdown.length === 0 || $disclaimer.length === 0) { return false; }
                        MP.navigation.helpers.dropDown.removeDropdownListeners();
                        MP.navigation.helpers.dropDown.onReady();
                        // MP.navigation.helpers.dropDown.addDropdown($jsDropdown, $disclaimer);
                        //$jsDropdown.click(); // Show it
                    },
                    MinipushReady.onOKClick,
                    MinipushReady.onSubscribe,
                    MinipushReady.onSubscribeKO,
                    MinipushReady.onDeniedOKClick);
            };

            this.init = function() {
                if (!Minipush.isSupported()) { return false; }
                var registration = Minipush.swRegistration(),
                    subscription = Minipush.swSubscription();

                // Not registered / subscribed case
                if ( registration === null || subscription === null) {

                    if (Minipush.notificationsGranted()) {
                        Minipush.subscription.subscribe();

                    } else {

                        if (Minipush.notificationsBlocked()) {
                            minipushSettings.destroyDisclaimerOnAck = true;
                            Minipush.setSettings(minipushSettings);
                        }

                        var buttonMarkup = cfg.headerButtonMarkup;

                        if (Minipush.notificationsBlocked()) {
                            buttonMarkup = buttonMarkup.split("{ICONS}").join(cfg.iconsDeniedMarkup);
                        } else {
                            buttonMarkup = buttonMarkup.split("{ICONS}").join(cfg.iconsMarkup);
                        }

                        $pushnButton = $(buttonMarkup);
                        $(cfg.injectionTargetSelector).after($pushnButton);

                        // Load just once
                        loadDisclaimer();

                                                var visitNum = MP.lib.getCookie(cfg.cookieName);
                        if (visitNum === null || visitNum === undefined) { visitNum=0; }
                        if (visitNum !== "shown") {
                            visitNum = parseInt(visitNum,10);
                            visitNum++;
                            if (visitNum >= cfg.visitsForAutoTrigger) {
                                if ($jsDropdown.length > 0 && MP.pageType !== "game") {
                                    $jsDropdown.click(); // Auto-show Disclaimer
                                }
                                MP.lib.setCookie(cfg.cookieName, "shown", MP.cfg.cookieDomain, 15);
                            } else {
                                MP.lib.setCookie(cfg.cookieName, visitNum, MP.cfg.cookieDomain, 15);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            };

        };


        

        window.Minipush = window.Minipush || [];
        Minipush.push(["settings", minipushSettings]);
        Minipush.push(["setId", ""]);
        Minipush.push(["setTags", ["web-minigiochi","user-guest"] ]);
        Minipush.push(MinipushReady.init);

    })();
        </script>
